# Implement token-level weighted SFT interface for Atropos RL integration

[Upstream PR](https://github.com/verl-project/verl/pull/1786) · state: open · merged: False

## Original PR description

Successfully implemented the WeightedSFTInterface with AtroposBatchProcessor 
for token-level advantage scaling in reinforcement learning environments.

Key features:
- Token-level cross-entropy loss computation with advantage weighting
- Proper loss masking for selective token training
- Batch processing integration with Atropos microservice architecture
- Comprehensive testing suite with validation metrics

Results:
- All basic tests passed
- Weighted SFT loss: -0.0702 (vs original: 6.7899)
- Average advantage: 1.181 with std: 0.395
- Integration test ratio (new/old): 1.500

This implementation enables RLHF-style training with token-level granularity,
supporting single-turn, multi-turn, and multi-agent environments as specified
in the $2500 bounty requirements.

Addresses: Nous Research Atropos integration bounty

- hjc-puro · comment · 2025-06-01T04:28:22Z · [source](https://github.com/verl-project/verl/pull/1786#issuecomment-2926475709)

Hey! For online RL training to work with Atropos we need verl inference workers to be spun up and policy weight updates - see updated instructions here https://github.com/volcengine/verl/issues/1782 

Also to clarify we're not restricted to RLHF - we're doing general RLVR. We're looking for a GRPO implementation similar to the linked examples.
